<script>

	
		
	 	setCookie('navegation_user', 'personas', 10);
		
	
	
	function setCookie(cname, cvalue, exdays) {
		var d = new Date();
		d.setTime(d.getTime() + (exdays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
		var expires = "expires="+d.toUTCString();
		document.cookie = cname + "=" + cvalue + ";" + expires + ";path=/";
	}

	function getCookie(cname) {
		var name = cname + "=";
		var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
		for(var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
		 var c = ca[i];
		 while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') {
			 c = c.substring(1);
		 }
		 if (c.indexOf(name) == 0) {
			 return c.substring(name.length, c.length);
		 }
		}
		return "";
	}
</script>

<script>
function callSearch(){
	var mainSearch = document.querySelector('.main-search');
	if(mainSearch !== null && mainSearch !== ''  && mainSearch!==undefined) {
	mainSearch.classList.remove('hidden');
	document.getElementById('searchBenefits').focus();
	document.getElementById('searchBenefits').click();
	}
}
</script>

<script src='https://www.bci.cl/beneficios/javascript/site-fuse_js-ffed7d281c50f085c34aa49f9767a31aa9775dd29b7779ae0b017002f1f91a69.js' type='text/javascript'></script>
<script src='https://www.bci.cl/beneficios/javascript/site-axiosjsonpadapter_js-ffed7d281c50f085c34aa49f9767a31aa9775dd29b7779ae0b017002f1f91a69.js' type='text/javascript'></script>
<script>
	var mainComponent = {
		name: 'mainComponent',
		delimiters: ['${', '}'],
		template: '#main-component',
		data: function() {
			return {
				content: [],
				results: [],
				inputSearch: '',
				searchWord: '',
				isVisible: false,
				suggestResults: [],
				option: '',
				comment: '',
				totalPages: 1,
				widthScreen: screen.width,
			};
		},
		created: function() {
			//this.getEntries();
			//this.getEntries();
			this.getEntryesByTag();
			window.addEventListener('resize', this.onResize);
		},
		unmounted: function(){
			this.inputSearch = '';
		},
		computed: {
			buscar: function() {
				
				var fuse = new Fuse( this.content, {
					keys:['searchText'],
					includeMatches: true,
					minMatchCharLength: 1,
					includeScore: 0,
					location: 0,
					distance: 200,
					threshold:0.6,
					ignoreLocation: true,
					findAllMatches: true,
				})
				//console.log('funciona');
				//_this.resultSearch = fuse.search(_this.inputSearch);
				const results = fuse.search(this.inputSearch)?.slice(0,6);
				searchInJson();
				return results;
			},
			searchSuggestedResults: function(){
				var _this = this;
				return _this.suggestResults;
			},
			calculateTextInInput: function(){
				return this.widthScreen >= 768 ? '¿Qué estás buscando en Bci?': '';
			}
		},
		methods: {
			getEntries: function () {
				var _this = this;
				var currentPage = 1;
				var apicall = function( entries ){
					axios.get('https://www.bci.cl/api/content/spaces/buscador/types/resultado/entries',{
							params: {
								per_page: 100,
								page: currentPage,
							}
					})
					.then(function (response) {
							try {
								var accEntries = entries.concat(response.data.entries);
								currentPage += 1;
								if (response.data.meta.total_pages >= currentPage) {
									apicall( accEntries );
								}
								else {
									_this.content = accEntries.map( element => {
																										return {
																											...element,
																											searchText: element.fields['Texto para busqueda'], 
																										}
																								});
									localStorage.setItem('entries', JSON.stringify( _this.content ));
								}
							} catch (e) {}

					})
					.catch(function (error) {
							console.log(error);
					});
				
			};
			apicall([]);

		},
			getEntryesByTag: function(){
				var _this = this;
				axios.get('https://www.bci.cl/api/content/spaces/buscador/types/resultado/entries?meta.tags=resultado-sugerido')
					.then( function(response){
					_this.suggestResults = response.data.entries;
				})
			},
			closeSearch: function(){
				var searchModal = document.querySelector('.main-search');
				searchModal.classList.add('hidden');
				this.inputSearch = '';
			},
			onResize: function(){
				this.widthScreen = screen.width;
			},
			showModal: function(){
				this.showOrHideComments();
			},
			submitComments:function(){
				//this.showOrHideThanksModal();
			},
			returnToSearch: function(){
				this.showOrHideThanksModal();
			},
			showOrHideComments: function(){
				const commentsModal = document.getElementById('comments');
				commentsModal.classList.toggle('showModal');
			},
			showOrHideThanksModal: function(){
				const thanksModal = document.getElementById('thanks');
	 			thanksModal.classList.toggle('thanks__show');
			},
			closeComments: function(){
				this.showOrHideComments();
			},
			closeThanksModal(){
				this.showOrHideComments();
				this.showOrHideThanksModal();
			},
			redirectToResults: function (){
				localStorage.setItem('lastSearch', this.inputSearch);
				window.location.href= `https://www.bci.cl/beneficios/mas-resultados?search=${this.inputSearch}`;
				this.inputSearch = '';
			},
			redirectToPageEntry: function( urlRedirect ){
				this.inputSearch = "";
				location.href = `https://${ urlRedirect }`;
			}
		}
	}

	 var appVue = new Vue({
		 name: 'appVue',
		 el: '#app-vue',
		 render: function(h) {
			 return h(mainComponent);
		 }
	 });
</script>

<script>
	function searchInJson(){	
	$(".dropdown__search > li > a").on("click", function(){
		var texto_buscar = $(this).text();
		dataLayer.push({
			'evento-interactivo-categoria' : "Buscador",
			'evento-interactivo-accion' : "Resultado Busqueda",
			'evento-interactivo-etiqueta' : texto_buscar,
			'event' :'evento-interactivo'
		});
	});
	}
	
	$("input.searchBuscador").keypress(function(e) {
		var code = (e.keyCode ? e.keyCode : e.which);
		if(code==13){
			var texto_buscar = $(this).val();
			dataLayer.push({
				'evento-interactivo-categoria' : "Buscador",
				'evento-interactivo-accion' : "Busqueda",
				'evento-interactivo-etiqueta' : texto_buscar,
				'event' :'evento-interactivo'
			});
		}
	});
	$("searchCerrar").on("click", function(){
		dataLayer.push({
			'evento-interactivo-categoria' : "Buscador",
			'evento-interactivo-accion' : "Clic",
			'evento-interactivo-etiqueta' : "Cerrar Buscador",
			'event' :'evento-interactivo'
		});
	});
</script>

<script>
	var widgetBuilderReady = function	(){
	setTimeout(function(){
		var loader = document.querySelector('.loadersite')
			if (loader) loader.remove();
			$('#main-layout').fadeIn();
		}, 400);
	};
	var elems = document.querySelectorAll('.widget-definition');
	if(elems.length === 0){
		widgetBuilderReady();
	}
</script>

	<script>
		(function(){
			/* Landing Beneficios del mes Bci · landing-beneficios.js · versión 1d76f5a

var HOY = KEYS[new Date().getDay()];
var ALIAS = {dia:'day', tarjeta:'card', categoria:'cat', formato:'fmt', rubro:'cat', tipo:'tipo', comuna:'comuna'};
function norm(s){ return String(s || '').toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, ''); }
function each(sel, fn, root){ Array.prototype.forEach.call((root || D).querySelectorAll(sel), fn); }
function push(cat, act, lab){ W.dataLayer.push({event:'beneficios_interaccion', eventCategory:cat || 'beneficios', eventAction:act, eventLabel:lab}); }
var params = null;
try { params = new URLSearchParams(W.location.search); } catch (e) {}
function param(k){ return params ? params.get(k) : null; }
if (!W.__vpGtm) {
W.__vpGtm = true;
D.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
var el = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-vp-gtm-action]');
if (!el || el.tagName === 'INPUT' || el.tagName === 'SELECT') return;
if (el.tagName === 'DETAILS' && !e.target.closest('summary')) return;

});
D.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
var el = e.target;
if (el.tagName === 'SELECT' && el.hasAttribute('data-vp-gtm-action')) push('beneficios', el.getAttribute('data-vp-gtm-action'), el.value);
});
}
if (!W.__vpTop) {
W.__vpTop = true;
var raf = 0;
var medir = function () {
raf = 0;
var alto = 0;
Array.prototype.forEach.call(D.querySelectorAll('header, nav, [class*="header"], [class*="Header"], [class*="navbar"], [id*="header"]'), function (el) {
if (el.closest('.vp')) return;
var cs = W.getComputedStyle(el);
if (cs.position !== 'fixed' && cs.position !== 'sticky') return;
if (cs.display === 'none' || cs.visibility === 'hidden' || +cs.opacity === 0) return;
var r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
if (r.top > 2 || r.bottom <= 0 || r.height > W.innerHeight * 0.4 || r.width < W.innerWidth * 0.5) return;
if (r.bottom > alto) alto = r.bottom;
});
var raiz = D.documentElement, nav = D.querySelector('.vp-nav');
raiz.style.setProperty('--vp-top', Math.round(alto) + 'px');
raiz.style.setProperty('--vp-offset', Math.round(alto + (nav ? nav.offsetHeight : 0) + 8) + 'px');
};
var pedir = function () { if (!raf) raf = W.requestAnimationFrame(medir); };
W.addEventListener('scroll', pedir, {passive: true});
W.addEventListener('resize', pedir);
W.addEventListener('load', pedir);
medir();
setTimeout(medir, 600); setTimeout(medir, 2000);
}
each('[data-vp-nav]', function (nav) {
if (nav.__vpObservar) { nav.__vpObservar(); return; }
var links = nav.querySelectorAll('a[href^="#"]');
if (!('IntersectionObserver' in W) || !links.length) return;
var map = {};
Array.prototype.forEach.call(links, function (a) { map[a.getAttribute('href').slice(1)] = a; });
var io = new IntersectionObserver(function (ents) {
ents.forEach(function (en) {
if (!en.isIntersecting) return;
Array.prototype.forEach.call(links, function (a) { a.classList.remove('is-active'); a.removeAttribute('aria-current'); });
var a = map[en.target.id];
if (!a) return;
a.classList.add('is-active'); a.setAttribute('aria-current', 'true');
var lista = a.closest('ul');
if (lista && lista.scrollTo) lista.scrollTo({left: a.parentNode.offsetLeft - 16, behavior: 'smooth'});
});
}, {rootMargin: '-45% 0px -50% 0px'});
nav.__vpObservar = function () {
Object.keys(map).forEach(function (id) {
var s = D.getElementById(id);
if (s && !s.__vpObs) { s.__vpObs = true; io.observe(s); }
});
};
nav.__vpObservar();
W.addEventListener('load', nav.__vpObservar);
});
W.__vpRes = W.__vpRes || {};
each('[data-vp-search]', function (form) {
if (form.__vpOk) return; form.__vpOk = true;
var input = form.querySelector('input'), out = D.getElementById(form.getAttribute('data-vp-search'));
var timer, ultimo = '';
function enviar(q, medir) {
W.__vpQ = q;
D.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('vp:search', {detail: {q: q}}));
if (medir && q && q !== ultimo) { push('beneficios', 'buscar', q); ultimo = q; }
}
function pintar() {
if (!out) return;
var q = input.value.trim();
if (!q) { out.textContent = ''; return; }
var partes = [];
Object.keys(W.__vpRes).forEach(function (k) { var r = W.__vpRes[k]; if (r.n) partes.push(r); });
if (!partes.length) { out.textContent = 'Sin resultados para «' + q + '». Prueba con otra palabra o comuna.'; return; }
out.innerHTML = '';
out.appendChild(D.createTextNode('Encontramos '));
partes.forEach(function (r, i) {
if (i) out.appendChild(D.createTextNode(i === partes.length - 1 ? ' y ' : ', '));
var a = D.createElement('a');
a.href = '#' + r.id;
a.textContent = r.n + ' ' + (r.n === 1 ? r.uno : r.varios);
out.appendChild(a);
});
}
D.addEventListener('vp:results', pintar);
input.addEventListener('input', function () { clearTimeout(timer); timer = setTimeout(function () { enviar(input.value.trim(), false); }, 200); });
form.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
e.preventDefault();
var q = input.value.trim();
enviar(q, true);
var dest = null;
Object.keys(W.__vpRes).some(function (k) { if (W.__vpRes[k].n) { dest = D.getElementById(W.__vpRes[k].id); return true; } return false; });
if (q && dest) dest.scrollIntoView({behavior: 'smooth'});
});
var q0 = param('q');
if (q0) { input.value = q0; setTimeout(function () { enviar(q0, false); }, 0); }
});
each('[data-vp-filter]', function (sec) {
if (sec.__vpOk) return; sec.__vpOk = true;
var st = {q: W.__vpQ || '', exp: false, orden: 'rec'};
var grupos = {};
each('[data-vp-f]', function (c) {
var g = c.getAttribute('data-vp-f');
(grupos[g] = grupos[g] || []).push(c);
st[g] = 'all';
}, sec);
var items = Array.prototype.slice.call(sec.querySelectorAll('.vp-item'));
items.forEach(function (li, i) { li.__i = i; li.__t = norm(li.getAttribute('data-text')); li.__v = {}; });
var tabs = Array.prototype.slice.call(sec.querySelectorAll('[role="tab"]'));
var panes = Array.prototype.slice.call(sec.querySelectorAll('[data-vp-pane]'));
var unidad = (sec.getAttribute('data-vp-unidad') || 'beneficio|beneficios').split('|');
var sinBusqueda = sec.hasAttribute('data-vp-sinbusqueda');
function vals(li, g) { if (!li.__v[g]) li.__v[g] = (li.getAttribute('data-' + g) || '').split('|'); return li.__v[g]; }
function cumple(li, g, v) {
if (v === 'all') return true;
if (v === 'today') v = HOY;
var a = vals(li, g);
return a.indexOf(v) >= 0 || (g === 'day' && a.indexOf('todos') >= 0);
}
function calza(li, salvo) {
for (var g in grupos) if (g !== salvo && !cumple(li, g, st[g])) return false;
return !st.q || li.__t.indexOf(norm(st.q)) >= 0;
}
function panelActivo() { for (var i = 0; i < panes.length; i++) if (!panes[i].hidden) return panes[i]; return sec; }
function enAlcance() { var p = panelActivo(); return items.filter(function (li) { return p.contains(li); }); }
function filtrando() { if (st.q) return true; for (var g in grupos) if (st[g] !== 'all') return true; return false; }
function llenarSelect(sel, g, alcance) {
var cuenta = {}, total = 0;
alcance.forEach(function (li) {
if (!calza(li, g)) return;
total++;
vals(li, g).forEach(function (v) { if (v) cuenta[v] = (cuenta[v] || 0) + 1; });
});
if (st[g] !== 'all' && !cuenta[st[g]]) cuenta[st[g]] = 0;
var claves = Object.keys(cuenta).sort(function (a, b) { return a.localeCompare(b, 'es'); });
var html = '<option value="all">' + sel.getAttribute('data-vp-todos') + ' (' + total + ')</option>' +
claves.map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + k.replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + '"' + (cuenta[k] ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' + k + ' (' + cuenta[k] + ')</option>'; }).join('');
if (sel.__html !== html) { sel.innerHTML = html; sel.__html = html; }
sel.value = st[g];
var fila = sel.closest('.vp-campo');
if (fila) fila.hidden = claves.length < 2 && st[g] === 'all';
}
function aplicar() {
var alcance = enAlcance();
Object.keys(grupos).forEach(function (g) {
grupos[g].forEach(function (c) {
c.classList.toggle('is-activo', st[g] !== 'all');
if (c.tagName === 'SELECT' && c.hasAttribute('data-vp-todos')) { llenarSelect(c, g, alcance); return; }
if (c.tagName === 'SELECT') {
Array.prototype.forEach.call(c.options, function (o) {
var k = 0;
alcance.forEach(function (li) { if (calza(li, g) && cumple(li, g, o.value)) k++; });
o.textContent = (o.value === 'today' ? 'Hoy, ' + NOMBRES[HOY] : o.getAttribute('data-l')) + ' (' + k + ')';
o.disabled = k === 0 && st[g] !== o.value;
});
c.value = st[g];
return;
}
var v = c.getAttribute('data-vp-v'), n = 0;
alcance.forEach(function (li) { if (calza(li, g) && cumple(li, g, v)) n++; });
var s = c.querySelector('[data-vp-n]');
if (s) s.textContent = n;
c.disabled = n === 0 && st[g] !== v;
c.setAttribute('aria-pressed', st[g] === v ? 'true' : 'false');
});
});
var filt = filtrando(), total = 0;
each('[data-vp-list]', function (ul) {

var lis = Array.prototype.slice.call(ul.children);
lis.forEach(function (li) { var ok = calza(li); li.hidden = !ok; if (ok) n++; });
var plegar = !filt && !st.exp && n > lim, k = 0;
lis.forEach(function (li) { if (!li.hidden) { li.classList.toggle('vp-extra', plegar && k >= lim); k++; } });
var caja = ul.closest('[data-vp-pane]') || sec;
var mas = caja.querySelector('[data-vp-more]');
if (mas) { mas.hidden = !plegar; mas.textContent = mas.getAttribute('data-vp-more').replace('{n}', n); }
var vacio = caja.querySelector('[data-vp-empty]');
if (vacio) vacio.hidden = n > 0;
var pane = ul.closest('[data-vp-pane]');
if (pane) { var t = sec.querySelector('[aria-controls="' + pane.id + '"] [data-vp-n]'); if (t) t.textContent = n; }
total += n;
});
var estado = sec.querySelector('[data-vp-status]');
if (estado) {

estado.textContent = filt ? (vis === 1 ? '1 ' + unidad[0] : vis + ' ' + unidad[1]) + ' con estos filtros' : '';
}
var lim = sec.querySelector('[data-vp-clear]');
if (lim) lim.hidden = !filt;
if (sinBusqueda) return;
W.__vpRes[sec.id] = {id: sec.id, n: total, uno: unidad[0], varios: unidad[1]};
D.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('vp:results', {detail: {scope: sec.id, count: total}}));
}
function ordenar() {
each('[data-vp-list]', function (ul) {
var lis = Array.prototype.slice.call(ul.children);
lis.sort(function (a, b) {
if (st.orden === 'pct') return (+b.getAttribute('data-pct') || 0) - (+a.getAttribute('data-pct') || 0) || a.__i - b.__i;
if (st.orden === 'az') return String(a.getAttribute('data-name')).localeCompare(b.getAttribute('data-name'), 'es');
return a.__i - b.__i;
}).forEach(function (li) { ul.appendChild(li); });
}, sec);
}
function activar(tab, foco) {
tabs.forEach(function (t) {
var on = t === tab;
t.setAttribute('aria-selected', on ? 'true' : 'false');
t.tabIndex = on ? 0 : -1;
var p = D.getElementById(t.getAttribute('aria-controls'));
if (p) p.hidden = !on;
});
if (foco) tab.focus();
if (grupos.comuna) st.comuna = 'all';
if (tab.getAttribute('data-dia')) push('beneficios', 'tab_dia', tab.getAttribute('data-dia'));
st.exp = false;
aplicar();
}
tabs.forEach(function (t, i) {
t.addEventListener('click', function () { activar(t); });
t.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
var j = {ArrowRight: i + 1, ArrowLeft: i - 1, Home: 0, End: tabs.length - 1}[e.key];
if (j === undefined) return;
e.preventDefault();
activar(tabs[(j + tabs.length) % tabs.length], true);
});
});
sec.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
var b = e.target.closest('button');
if (!b || !sec.contains(b) || b.disabled) return;
if (b.hasAttribute('data-vp-f')) { st[b.getAttribute('data-vp-f')] = b.getAttribute('data-vp-v'); st.exp = false; aplicar(); }
else if (b.hasAttribute('data-vp-clear')) { for (var g in grupos) st[g] = 'all'; aplicar(); }
else if (b.hasAttribute('data-vp-more')) {
var caja = b.closest('[data-vp-pane]') || sec;
var primero = caja.querySelector('.vp-extra');
st.exp = true; aplicar();
if (primero) { primero.tabIndex = -1; primero.focus({preventScroll: true}); }
}
});
sec.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
var el = e.target;
if (el.hasAttribute('data-vp-f')) { st[el.getAttribute('data-vp-f')] = el.value; aplicar(); }
else if (el.hasAttribute('data-vp-sort')) { st.orden = el.value; ordenar(); aplicar(); }
});
D.addEventListener('vp:search', function (e) {
if (sinBusqueda) return;
st.q = e.detail.q || '';
aplicar();
if (st.q && tabs.length && !panelActivo().querySelector('.vp-item:not([hidden])')) {
var otro = panes.filter(function (p) { return items.some(function (li) { return p.contains(li) && calza(li); }); })[0];
if (otro) activar(sec.querySelector('[aria-controls="' + otro.id + '"]'));
}
});
if (params) params.forEach(function (v, k) {
var g = ALIAS[k] || k;
if (!grupos[g]) return;
v = v === 'hoy' ? 'today' : v;
if (grupos[g].some(function (c) { return c.tagName === 'SELECT' || c.getAttribute('data-vp-v') === v; })) st[g] = v;
});
var hoyBtn = sec.querySelector('[data-vp-v="today"] [data-vp-hoy]');
if (hoyBtn) hoyBtn.textContent = 'Hoy, ' + NOMBRES[HOY];
var tabHoy = tabs.filter(function (t) { return t.getAttribute('data-dia') === HOY; })[0];
if (tabHoy) { var marca = tabHoy.querySelector('[data-vp-hoy]'); if (marca) marca.hidden = false; }
if (tabs.length) activar(tabHoy || tabs.filter(function (t) { return t.getAttribute('aria-selected') === 'true'; })[0] || tabs[0]);
else aplicar();
});
};
  function correr() { try { iniciar(); } catch (e) { if (window.console) console.warn('landing-beneficios', e); } }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', correr); else correr();
  window.addEventListener('load', correr);
  if ('MutationObserver' in window) {
    var t, mo = new MutationObserver(function () { clearTimeout(t); t = setTimeout(correr, 150); });
    mo.observe(document.documentElement, {childList: true, subtree: true});
    setTimeout(function () { mo.disconnect(); }, 20000);
  }
})();

		})();
	</script>

<div class="vp-info__bloque"><h3>Condiciones generales</h3><p>Los beneficios del mes valen hasta el 1 de octubre de 2026, salvo que el beneficio indique otra vigencia. Cada uno aplica solo el día, con la tarjeta y en el formato (presencial u online) que se indica, y está sujeto a las condiciones de cada comercio.</p></div>
<div class="vp-info__bloque"><h3>Topes y exclusiones</h3><p>Los topes de descuento van de $&nbsp;8.000 a $&nbsp;100.000 según el beneficio, y 71 no tienen tope. Revisa la condición de cada comercio antes de pagar.</p></div>

<script>
	let openedMenu = false;

	function onchangeMenu() {
			document.getElementById("intro-step1").addEventListener("click", () => {
					if (!openedMenu) {
							openModalNMH();
					} else {
							closeModalNMH();
							dataLayer.push({
									event: 'ev-int',
									'evento-interactive-accion': 'Cerrar Menú',
									'evento-interactive-categoria': 'mh nv',
									'evento-interactive-etiqueta': 'Cerrar Menú'
							});
					}
			});

			document.getElementById("outputMH").addEventListener("click", () => {
					closeModalNMH();
			});

			document.getElementById("closeNewMenuHamburguesa").addEventListener("click", () => {
					closeModalNMH();
			});
	}

	function detectTabFocus() {
			const menu = document.getElementById('newMenuHamburguesa');
			document.addEventListener('focusin', function (e) {
					if (!menu.contains(e.target) && e.target.id != "intro-step1" && e.target.id != "fullNMHDesktop") {
							closeModalNMH();
					}
			});
	}

		function openModalNMH() {
				openedMenu = true;

				document.querySelector('#intro-step1 > span').classList.remove('icon-bci-menu-outline');
				document.querySelector('#intro-step1 > span').classList.add('icon-bci-close-outline');

				const span = document.querySelector('#intro-step1 > span');
				span.style.fontSize = '20px';
				span.style.marginTop = '-4px';
				span.style.marginRight = '5px';

				const scrollTop = document.documentElement.scrollTop || document.body.scrollTop;
				if (scrollTop === 0) {
						document.getElementById("newMenuHamburguesa").style.top = "105px";
				} else {
						document.getElementById("newMenuHamburguesa").style.top = "70px";
				}
			
				document.getElementById("newMenuHamburguesa").classList.remove('d-none');

				document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';

				moverSlider();
				arrowMenu();
				detectTabFocus();

				const firstOptionMH = document.getElementById('firstOptionMH');
				if (firstOptionMH) {
						firstOptionMH.focus();
				}
		}


		function closeModalNMH() {
				document.body.style.overflow = 'scroll';

				openedMenu = false;

				document.querySelector('#intro-step1 > span').classList.remove('icon-bci-close-outline');
				document.querySelector('#intro-step1 > span').classList.add('icon-bci-menu-outline');
				document.getElementById("newMenuHamburguesa").classList.add('d-none');
				const span = document.querySelector('#intro-step1 > span');
				span.removeAttribute('style');
		}

    window.changeContMenuBCI = function(idMenu, nameMenu, eventOrLink) {
				var titleElem = document.querySelector("#txtMenuSelect");
				if (titleElem) {
						titleElem.innerHTML = nameMenu;
				}

				var menuContainer = document.getElementById('containersMenuMundos');
				if (menuContainer && menuContainer.children) {
						var opciones = menuContainer.children;
						for (var i = 0; i < opciones.length; i++) {
								if (opciones[i].id === idMenu) {
										opciones[i].classList.remove('d-none');
								} else {
										opciones[i].classList.add('d-none');
								}
						}
				}

				var navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('#optMenuLatPersonas .new-modal__menu-icon');
				navLinks.forEach(function (link) {
						if (link && link.classList) {
								link.classList.remove('active');
						}
				});

				var clickedLink = null;
				if (eventOrLink) {
						if (eventOrLink.nodeType) {
								clickedLink = eventOrLink;
						} else if (eventOrLink.currentTarget) {
								clickedLink = eventOrLink.currentTarget;
						} else if (eventOrLink.target) {
								clickedLink = eventOrLink.target.closest('a');
						}
				}

				if (clickedLink && clickedLink.classList) {
						clickedLink.classList.add('active');
				}
		};

		 window.changeMenuBCI = function(idMenu, event) {
					var menuContainer = document.getElementById('menuMundoPersonas');
					if (!menuContainer) return;

					var opciones = menuContainer.children;
					for (var i = 0; i < opciones.length; i++) {
							if (opciones[i].id === idMenu) {
									opciones[i].classList.remove('d-none');
							} else {
									opciones[i].classList.add('d-none');
							}
					}

					var navLinks = document.querySelectorAll('#optMenuPersonas .new-nav-link');
					navLinks.forEach(function (link) {
							link.classList.remove('active');
							var span = link.querySelector('span');
							if (span) {
									span.classList.remove('color-blue-normal');
							}
					});

					var clickedLink = null;
					if (event) {
							clickedLink = event.currentTarget || (event.target ? event.target.closest('a') : null);
					}

					if (clickedLink && clickedLink.classList) {
							clickedLink.classList.add('active');

							var clickedSpan = clickedLink.querySelector('span');
							if (clickedSpan && clickedSpan.classList) {
									clickedSpan.classList.add('color-blue-normal');
							}
					}
			};

    function arrowMenu() {
        const menu = document.getElementById('cont-optMenuPersonas');
        const arrowLeft = document.getElementById('arrowLeft');
        const arrowRight = document.getElementById('arrowRight');
        const scrollAmount = 190;

        function updateArrows() {
            if (menu.scrollLeft <= 0) {
                arrowLeft.classList.add('hidden');
            } else {
                arrowLeft.classList.remove('hidden');
            }
            if (menu.scrollLeft + menu.clientWidth >= menu.scrollWidth - 2) {
                arrowRight.classList.add('hidden');
            } else {
                arrowRight.classList.remove('hidden');
            }
        }

        arrowLeft.addEventListener('click', function () {
            menu.scrollBy({ left: -scrollAmount, behavior: 'smooth' });
            window.updateSliderPosition;
        });

        arrowRight.addEventListener('click', function () {
            menu.scrollBy({ left: scrollAmount, behavior: 'smooth' });
            window.updateSliderPosition;
        });

        menu.addEventListener('scroll', function () {
            updateArrows();
            window.updateSliderPosition();
        });

        window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
            updateArrows();
            window.updateSliderPosition();
        });

        updateArrows();
    }

    function moverSlider() {
        const slider = document.getElementById('slider');
        const links = document.querySelectorAll('#optMenuPersonas .new-nav-link');
        const ul = document.getElementById('optMenuPersonas');

        function moveSlider(el) {
            const ulRect = ul.getBoundingClientRect();
            const elRect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
            const left = elRect.left - ulRect.left + ul.scrollLeft;
            slider.style.width = el.offsetWidth + 'px';
            slider.style.left = left + 'px';
        }

        links.forEach(link => {
            link.addEventListener('click', e => {
                links.forEach(l => l.classList.remove('active'));
                link.classList.add('active');
                moveSlider(link);
            });
        });

        window.updateSliderPosition = function () {
            const activeLink = document.querySelector('#optMenuPersonas .new-nav-link.active');
            if (activeLink) {
                moveSlider(activeLink);
            }
        };

        window.updateSliderPosition();

        window.addEventListener('resize', window.updateSliderPosition);
    }

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
        moverSlider();
        arrowMenu();
        metricasMHMCP();
				onchangeMenu();
    });

    function metricasMHMCP() {
        document.querySelector('#newMenuHamburguesa').addEventListener('click', function (e) {

            const link = e.target.closest('a');
            const dismissButton = e.target.closest('#closeNewMenuHamburguesa');

            let accion = 'Clickthrough';
            let etiqueta = '';

            if (link) {
                etiqueta = link.innerText.trim();
            } else if (dismissButton) {
                accion = 'Dismiss';
                etiqueta = 'Cerrar Menú';
            } else {
                return;
            }

            dataLayer.push({
                event: 'ev-int',
                'evento-interactive-accion': accion,
                'evento-interactive-categoria': 'mh nv',
                'evento-interactive-etiqueta': etiqueta
            });
        });
    }

    function defaultSelect() {
        const menuContainer = document.getElementById('optMenuPersonas');
        if (!menuContainer) return;

        const pathParts = window.location.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean);
        const paramURL = pathParts[0];
        const paramURLEspecial = pathParts[1];

        const ariaLabelByParam = {
            "tarjetas": "Tarjetas",
            "creditos-de-consumo": "Crédito Consumo",
            "credito-hipotecario": "Hipotecario",

            "corredora-de-seguros": "Seguros",
            "beneficios": "Beneficios",
            "inversiones": "Inversiones"
        };

        function clickMenuOption(label) {
            const link = menuContainer.querySelector(`a[aria-label="${label}"]`);
            if (link) link.click();
        }

        if (paramURL && ariaLabelByParam[paramURL]) {
            clickMenuOption(ariaLabelByParam[paramURL]);
        }

        else if (paramURLEspecial && ariaLabelByParam[paramURLEspecial]) {
            clickMenuOption(ariaLabelByParam[paramURLEspecial]);
        }

        const currentFullURL = window.location.href;
        const links = document.querySelectorAll('a.optInfMenu');

        links.forEach(link => {
            const linkHref = new URL(link.getAttribute('href'), window.location.origin).href;

            if (linkHref === currentFullURL) {
                link.classList.add('active');
            } else {
                link.classList.remove('active');
            }
        });

    }

					],

					description: 'En Bci reportamos nuestro desempeño económico, social y ambiental desde el año 2004, situándonos entre las mejores empresas en reputación corporativa, RSE, innovación, y transparencia.'
				},
				acercaDeBci: {
					class: 'modal__acerca-de-bci',
					name: 'Acerca de Bci',
					homeUrl: '',
					links: [
						{ title: 'Presencia internacional', url: 'https://www.bci.cl/personas/presencia-internacional' },
{ title: 'Investor relations', url: 'https://www.bci.cl/investor-relations' },
{ title: 'Transparencia', url: 'https://www.bci.cl/sostenibilidad/transparencia-y-confianza' },
{ title: 'Sostenibilidad', url: 'https://www.bci.cl/sostenibilidad' },						
{ title: 'Sala de prensa', url: 'https://www.bci.cl/saladeprensa' },							
					],
					cols: 'col-4 col-md-3 col-lg-4',
					description: 'Te invitamos a hacer juntos tu vida más simple. Conoce las diferentes soluciones financieras que tenemos para ti.'
				},	
				current: null,
				currentPage: window.location.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, ''),
				currentSite: null,
				showModal: false,
				isDesktop: null,
				bodyTag: null
			}
		},
		created: function() {
			var self = this;
				self.checkDevice();
				self.setTabDefault();
				eventBus.$on('toggle-modal', function() {
					self.openModal();
				})
				self.bodyTag = document.querySelector('body[class*="context"]');
				self.getCurrentSite();
		},
		methods: {
			getCurrentSite: function() {
				var self = this;
				var url = window.location.pathname.split('/');
				self.currentSite = '/' + url[1];
			},
			changeMenuBCI: function(idMenu, event) {
            if (typeof window.changeMenuBCI === 'function') {
                window.changeMenuBCI(idMenu, event);
            }
      },
			changeContMenuBCI: function(idMenu, nameMenu, clickedLink) {
            if (typeof window.changeContMenuBCI === 'function') {
                window.changeContMenuBCI(idMenu, nameMenu, clickedLink);
            }
      },
			checkDevice: function() {
				var screenSize = window.innerWidth;
				if(this.isIE){
					this.isDesktop = false;
				}
				else if(screenSize > 991) {
					this.isDesktop = true;
				}
				else {
					this.isDesktop = false;
				}
			},
			openModal: function() {
				var self = this;
				self.showModal = !self.showModal; 

				if(self.isDesktop) {
					self.setTab(self.current.name);
				}
			},
			closeModal: function() {
				var self = this;
				self.showModal = false;
				closeModalNMH();
			},
			setTabDefault: function() {
				var self = this;
				if(this.activeSite !== '') {
					self.setTabCustom('cookie', this.activeSite );
				}
				else {
					self.setTabCustom('site', this.currentSite);
				}
			},
			setTabCustom: function(type, value) {
				var self = this;
				if(type === 'cookie') {
					switch (value) {
						case 'personas':
							self.current = self.personas;
							break;
						case 'empresarios':
							self.current = self.empresarios;
							break;
						default:
							self.setTabCustom('site', self.currentSite);
					} 
				}
				else if (type === 'site') {
					switch (value) {
						case self.personas.homeUrl:
							self.setTab(self.personas.name);
							break;
						case self.empresarios.homeUrl:
							self.setTab(self.empresarios.name);
							break;
						case self.empresas.homeUrl:
							self.setTab(self.empresas.name);
							break;
						case self.inversiones.homeUrl:
							self.setTab(self.inversiones.name);
							break;
						case self.acercaDeBci.homeUrl:
							self.setTab(self.acercaDeBci.name);
							break;
						default:
							self.setTab(self.personas.name);
					} 
				}
			},
			animateCSS: function (element, animation) {
				var self = this;
				var prefix = arguments.length > 2 && arguments[2] !== undefined ? arguments[2] : 'animate__';
				return (// We create a Promise and return it
					new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
						var animationName = "".concat(prefix).concat(animation);
						var node = element;
						node.classList.add("".concat(prefix, "animated"), animationName); 

						function handleAnimationEnd() {
							node.classList.remove("".concat(prefix, "animated"), animationName);
							node.removeEventListener('animationend', handleAnimationEnd);
							resolve('Animation ended');
						}

						node.addEventListener('animationend', handleAnimationEnd);
					})
				);
			},
			animatedCards: function() {
				var self = this;
				var arrCards = this.$refs.cards.querySelectorAll('div:not(.animated__monologo):not(.etiqueta-card)');
				var totalCards = arrCards.length;
				arrCards.forEach(function(card, index) {
					if(index > (Math.round(totalCards/2) - 1)) {
						self.animateCSS(card, 'slow');
					}
					self.animateCSS(card, 'fadeInRight');
				});

			},
			animatedModal: function(primaryColor, secondaryColor) {
				var self = this;
				self.animateCSS(self.$refs.mono, 'fadeInRight');
				self.animateCSS(self.$refs.contentTab, 'fadeInLeft');
				self.animatedCards();
				if(self.current.name !== self.acercaDeBci.name) {
					self.$refs.bgCurveColor.style.fill = primaryColor;
					self.animateCSS(self.$refs.lineCurveColor, 'fadeInLeft');
					self.$refs.lineCurveColor.style.stroke = secondaryColor;
				}
				else {
					self.animateCSS(self.$refs.lineCurveBlue, 'fadeInLeft');
					self.animateCSS(self.$refs.lineCurveRed, 'fadeInLeft');
					self.animateCSS(self.$refs.lineCurveYellow, 'fadeInLeft');
					self.animateCSS(self.$refs.lineCurveGreen, 'fadeInLeft');
				}
			},
			setTab: function(option) {
				var self = this;

				switch (option) {
					case 'Personas':
						self.current = self.personas;
						//if(self.isDesktop) self.animatedModal('#28892B', '#53A155');
						break;
					case 'PYME':
						self.current = self.empresarios;
						//if(self.isDesktop) self.animatedModal('#F13D47', '#ED0C19');						
						break;
					case 'Empresas':
						window.location.href = 'https://www.bci.cl/empresas';
						break;
					case 'Inversiones':
						self.current = self.inversiones;
						//if(self.isDesktop) self.animatedModal('#FFE300', '#FFE933');
						break;
					case 'Acerca de Bci':
						self.current = self.acercaDeBci;
						//if(self.isDesktop) self.animatedModal();
						break;
					default:
						self.current = self.personas;
						//if(self.isDesktop) self.animatedModal('#28892B', '#53A155');
				} 
			}
		},
		computed: {
			isIE:function () {
				return /Trident\/|MSIE/.test(window.navigator.userAgent);
			},
			currentTab: function() {
				return this.current;
			}
		},
	});

	var appMenu = new Vue({
		name: 'AppMenu',
		el: '#app__menu',
		render: function(h) {
			return h(mainModal);
		}
	});
</script>
